TL;DR: 本文研究了基于滚动分裂共形预测（Rolling Split Conformal Prediction）的牵引力控制预警方法，通过监测每位车手随机森林滑移行为模型中非一致性残差的波动性，试图在牵引力完全丧失前提供统计意义上的预警信号。然而，在19位车手、55,563个测试阶段遥测样本上的实验结果为阴性：该方法对14个地面真实事件的精度和召回率均为0.0，同时错误标记了15.3%的样本，虚警率过高。残差自相关诊断表明所有车手的分裂共形可交换性假设均被违反，这是导致高虚警率的原因之一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统牵引力控制架构仅在轮胎附着力极限被突破后才进行干预，本文试图探索一种在牵引力完全丧失之前就能提供统计上严格的预警信号的方法，以实现提前干预。

Method: 采用滚动分裂共形预测（Rolling Split Conformal Prediction），监测来自每位车手随机森林滑移行为模型中非一致性残差的波动性。与早期内部草案相比，修正了滑移代理中的混淆因素（将车速作为显式模型特征），使用每位车手的每个赛车圈而非仅最快圈，并使用从FIA赛事控制消息和赛道限制圈速删除中提取的真实时间戳事件标签进行评估。

Result: 阴性结果：19位车手、55,563个测试样本上，滚动波动检测器对14个地面真实事件的均精度≈0.0，均召回率=0.0，同时平均标记15.3%的样本为异常。静态95百分位阈值基线表现同样不佳，无法证明共形波动公式的额外复杂性。所有车手的残差自相关诊断均违反分裂共形可交换性假设（Ljung-Box p < 0.001, n = 19/19）。

Conclusion: 所提出的滚动分裂共形预测方法无法作为有效的牵引力丧失前预警信号，高虚警率源于残差自相关导致的可交换性假设违反。本文报告了这一方法学上严格的阴性发现，诊断了可能的原因，并概述了构建真正可预测版本所需的条件。

Abstract: Conventional traction control architectures intervene only after the adhesion limit of a tire has already been breached. This paper investigates whether Rolling Split Conformal Prediction , monitoring the volatility of non-conformity residuals from a per-driver Random Forest model of expected slip behavior , can serve as a statistically grounded pre-incident warning signal, ahead of gross traction loss. Unlike an earlier internal draft of this work, the evaluation reported here corrects a confound in the slip proxy (vehicle speed is included as an explicit model feature, not left implicit in the target's denominator), uses every racing lap for each driver rather than only the fastest lap, and is scored against real, timestamped incident labels extracted from FIA Race Control Messages and track-limits lap deletions rather than narrated post-hoc. The result is negative: across 19 drivers and 55,563 test-phase telemetry samples, the rolling-volatility detector achieves a mean precision of essentially 0.0 and mean recall of 0.0 against 14 ground-truth incidents, while flagging on average 15.3% of all samples as anomalous , too high a false-alarm rate for any early-warning use. A static 95th-percentile threshold baseline performs no better in any way that would justify the added complexity of the conformal-volatility formulation. Residual autocorrelation diagnostics show the split-conformal exchangeability assumption is violated for every driver (Ljung-Box p < 0.001, n = 19/19), which is one plausible driver of the high false-alarm rate. We report this as a methodologically rigorous negative finding, diagnose its likely causes, and outline what a genuinely predictive version of this approach would require.

</details>


### [174] [ART for Diffusion Sampling: Continuous-Time Control and Actor-Critic Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02137)
*Yilie Huang,Wenpin Tang,Xun Yu Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ART-RL，一种通过强化学习为扩散模型采样自适应分配时间步的方法，将时间步分配建模为连续时间控制问题，并证明其与原始ART优化问题在最优策略层面等价。实验表明该方法可即插即用于现有扩散采样器，一致提升样本质量，且学到的调度具有跨预算、数据集、求解器和表示空间的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散模型采样使用固定均匀或手工设计的时间步调度，这些预设方案可能是次优的，缺乏自适应分配时间步的能力。

Method: 提出ART（自适应重参数化时间），将扩散时间轴的速度视为控制变量，通过连续时间控制框架学习时间变换，使均匀网格在学到的时钟上产生自适应时间步。进一步提出ART-RL，将其转化为带高斯策略的连续时间强化学习问题，证明两者在优化器层面等价，并推导基于轨迹的矩恒等式以实现actor-critic更新。

Result: 在从低维可控设置到图像生成的实验中，ART-RL可即插即用于现有扩散采样器，仅改变时间步网格即可在相同预算下一致提升样本质量，超越强基线调度。学到的调度还展现出跨预算、数据集、求解器、流水线和表示空间的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 通过强化学习自适应学习扩散采样时间步分配是一种有效且通用的方法，可提升采样质量并具有良好的泛化性。

Abstract: We study timestep allocation for score-based diffusion sampling, where a learned reverse-time dynamics is discretized on a finite grid. Uniform and hand-crafted schedules are standard choices, but they rely on fixed prescriptions and can therefore be suboptimal. To address this limitation, we propose Adaptive Reparameterized Time (ART), a continuous-time control formulation that learns a time change by treating the speed of the sampling clock as the control, so that a uniform grid on the learned clock induces adaptive timesteps in the original diffusion time. Based on a leading-order Euler error surrogate, ART provides a principled objective for allocating timesteps along the sampling trajectory. To solve this deterministic control problem, we introduce ART-RL, an auxiliary randomized formulation with Gaussian policies that turns schedule learning into a continuous-time reinforcement learning problem. We prove that the randomized ART-RL formulation is equivalent to ART at the optimizer level, in the sense that its optimal Gaussian policy recovers the optimal ART time-warping rate through its mean. We further establish policy evaluation and policy improvement characterizations and derive trajectory-based moment identities that yield implementable actor--critic updates for learning the schedule. Across experiments ranging from controlled low-dimensional settings to image generation, ART-RL can be plugged into existing diffusion samplers by changing only the timestep grid, consistently improving sample quality over strong baseline schedules at matched budgets while leaving the rest of the sampling pipeline unchanged. The learned schedules also exhibit broad generalization, transferring without retraining across sampling budgets, datasets, solvers, pipelines, and representation spaces.

</details>


### [175] [Probing Chemical Language Models: Effects of Pre-training and Fine-tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02140)
*Anna Karnysheva,Dietrich Klakow,Ji-Ung Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了化学语言模型（CLMs）对78种分子子结构的编码能力，发现预训练能提升模型对分子结构的感知，尤其在上层；随机初始化模型的第一层已能良好编码环状结构；微调会更多地影响与任务相关的分子子结构，符合化学理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 化学语言模型虽被广泛使用，但其内部究竟编码了哪些具有化学意义的子结构尚不明确，因此需要系统性地探究CLMs对分子子结构的表征能力。

Method: 对8个预训练和6个随机初始化的化学语言模型进行了系统探测，针对78种分子子结构进行分析，并研究了在化学下游任务上微调对子结构表征的影响。

Result: 预训练提升了CLMs对分子结构的认知，尤其在上层表现明显；随机初始化模型的第一层已能良好编码环结构；微调对任务相关子结构的影响更大。

Conclusion: 化学语言模型通过预训练获得了更好的分子结构感知能力，且微调过程中表征的变化遵循化学理论，即任务相关的子结构受到更多影响。

Abstract: Chemical language models (CLMs) are trained with linearized representations such as SMILES, yet it remains unclear which chemically meaningful substructures they encode. To foster a better understanding of CLMs, we conduct a systematic study and probe for 78 molecular substructures across eight pre-trained and six randomly initialized models. We furthermore study how fine-tuning on chemical downstream tasks affects the learned representations of molecular substructures. Our results show that pre-training generally improves molecular structure awareness of CLMs, particularly in the upper layers. Moreover, randomly initialized models already encode ring structures well in the first layer. Our analysis on two chemical downstream tasks further reveals that, interestingly, fine-tuning affects task-relevant molecular substructures more than others, indicating that the changes in the representations follow chemical theory.

</details>


### [176] [Predicting Early Stages Of Alzheimer's Disease And Identifying Key Biomarkers Using Deep Artificial Neural Network And Ensemble Of Machine Learning Methodologies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02142)
*Debopriya Ghosh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于机器学习的阿尔茨海默病早期检测模型，使用ADNI数据集，通过迭代插补处理缺失值、Borderline SVM-SMOTE处理类别不平衡、包装法和嵌入法进行特征选择，构建了以逻辑回归、Extra Trees、Bagging KNN和LightGBM为基分类器的堆叠集成模型及人工神经网络，最终通过多种指标评估性能，旨在寻找最佳分类器并识别早期诊断的重要生物标志物。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 阿尔茨海默病早期症状轻微易与正常衰老混淆，导致多数患者确诊时病情已进展，且目前尚无治愈方法。早期检测有助于医生更好地管理病情并及时干预，因此亟需有效的早期诊断方法。

Method: 使用ADNI数据集，采用迭代插补处理缺失值，Borderline SVM-SMOTE解决类别不平衡，结合包装法和嵌入法进行特征选择；构建以Logistic Regression、Extra Trees、Bagging KNN和LightGBM为基分类器的堆叠集成模型，同时训练人工神经网络，通过精确率、召回率、F1分数和AUC-ROC评估性能。

Result: 堆叠集成模型与人工神经网络在阿尔茨海默病早期检测任务上进行了性能比较，通过多项指标评估分类效果，并识别出对早期诊断有重要贡献的生物标志物。

Conclusion: 本研究提出的机器学习方法能够有效实现阿尔茨海默病的早期检测，堆叠集成模型和人工神经网络均表现出良好性能，所识别的重要生物标志物可为临床早期诊断提供参考依据。

Abstract: Alzheimers disease (AD) is a brain disorder that develops slowly and mainly affects memory, thinking, language, and daily activities. It is one of the most common causes of dementia and creates many difficulties for patients as well as their families. In the early stage, the symptoms are often mild and may look like normal ageing. For this reason, many people are diagnosed late, when the disease has already progressed. At present, there is no complete cure for AD. Still, early detection can help doctors manage the condition better and take suitable steps at the right time. In this study, a machine learning model is proposed to detect the early stages of Alzheimers disease using clinical details, neuropsychological test scores, and neuroimaging-related measures. The data used in this work is collected from the Alzheimers Disease Neuroimaging Initiative (ADNI). As the dataset has missing values, iterative imputation is applied to fill them. The dataset also has class imbalance, which is handled using Borderline SVM-SMOTE. After that, feature selection is carried out using wrapper-based and embedded methods so that only important features are used for training. The selected features are divided into training and testing sets, and feature scaling is applied. A stacking ensemble model is developed using Logistic Regression, Extra Trees, Bagging KNN, and LightGBM as base classifiers. Along with this, an artificial neural network is also trained on the same dataset. The performance of these models is compared using precision, recall, F1-score, and AUC-ROC. This study aims to find the best classifier and also identify important biomarkers that may help in the early diagnosis of Alzheimers disease.

</details>


### [177] [Dynamic Neural Graph Encoding of Inference Processes in Deep Weight Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02166)
*Di Wu,Huan Liu,Zhixiang Chi,Yuanhao Yu,Konstantinos N. Plataniotis,Yang Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出动态图神经网络编码器(DNG-Encoder)来处理神经网络权重空间，通过将网络参数表示为动态图并捕获推理过程中的时序动态，在INR分类任务上比现有最优方法提升约10%的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在处理神经网络高维权重空间时效率低下，且忽略了神经网络逐层推理的时序特性。

Method: 提出DNG-Encoder，使用动态图表示神经网络参数以捕获推理的时序动态；并基于DNG-Encoder开发INR2JLS框架，将隐式神经表示映射到联合潜在空间以支持下游应用。

Result: 在CIFAR-100-INR数据集上，INR分类准确率比现有最优方法提升约10%。

Conclusion: 动态图方法能有效捕捉神经网络参数中的时序信息，显著提升权重空间分析任务的性能。

Abstract: The rapid advancements in using neural networks as implicit data representations have attracted significant interest in developing machine learning methods that analyze and process the weight spaces of other neural networks. However, efficiently handling these highdimensional weight spaces remains challenging. Existing methods often overlook the sequential nature of layer-by-layer processing in neural network inference. In this work, we propose a novel approach using dynamic graphs to represent neural network parameters, capturing the temporal dynamics of inference. Our Dynamic Neural Graph Encoder (DNG-Encoder) processes these graphs, preserving the sequential nature of neural processing. Additionally, we also leverage DNG-Encoder to develop INR2JLS (Implicit Neural Representation to Joint Latent Space) for facilitate downstream applications, such as classifying Implicit Neural Representations (INRs). Our approach demonstrates significant improvements across multiple tasks, surpassing the state-of-the-art INR classification accuracy by approximately 10% on the CIFAR-100-INR.

</details>


### [178] [Bayesian Sparse Low-Rank Adaptation for Large Language Model Uncertainty Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02182)
*Jijie Zhang,Zhe Ren,Quan Zhang,Dandan Guo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出DALorRA，一种基于变分贝叶斯的稀疏低秩自适应框架，通过在秩维度上施加随机掩码来校准大语言模型的过度自信问题，在不牺牲推理准确率的前提下显著提升不确定性校准质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在任务特定微调中普遍存在过度自信（overconfidence）问题，严重阻碍了可信部署。现有不确定性量化方法主要在密集参数空间中进行，计算开销大且效率低。

Method: 提出DALorRA框架：利用LoRA将多个秩一分量聚合的特性，在秩维度上施加随机掩码（stochastic masking），实现训练时的贝叶斯正则化（控制模型容量）和推理时的集成式校准（ensemble-like calibration）。

Result: 大量实验表明，DALorRA能够在不牺牲推理准确率的前提下，显著提升LLM的校准质量（calibration）。

Conclusion: 将不确定性量化的范式从密集参数空间转移至轻量级的秩级别是可行且有效的，为LLM可信部署提供了新思路。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) exhibit remarkable reasoning capabilities, but their task-specific fine-tuning is notoriously plagued by overconfidence, severely hindering trustworthy deployment. We propose Data-Adaptive Lower-Rank Adaptation (DALorRA), a simple and effective variational Bayesian sparse framework that shifts the paradigm of uncertainty quantification from the dense parameter space to the lightweight rank level of low-rank adaptation (LoRA). With the insight that LoRA essentially aggregates multiple rank-one components that may provide superfluous model capacity, DALorRA imposes stochastic masking on rank dimensions, enabling Bayesian regularization of model capacity during training and ensemble-like calibration during inference. Extensive experiments demonstrate DALorRA's excellent calibration of LLMs without compromising reasoning accuracy.

</details>


### [179] [Privacy-Preserving and Verifiable Approximate Distributed Coded Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02187)
*Xavier Martínez-Luaña,Alba Gude-Santos,Manuel Fernández-Veiga,Rebeca P. Díaz-Redondo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个模型无关的抗 adversary 分布式学习框架，首次将隐私保护（GPBACC 编码计算）与恶意行为防御（鲁棒聚合、近似解码比较、群组检测）统一应用于联邦学习和去中心化学习两种范式，并通过攻击驱动实验验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分布式机器学习防御方案通常独立处理隐私泄露和恶意操纵问题，且多针对特定学习范式或模型架构设计，缺乏统一的防御框架；联邦学习与去中心化学习具有截然不同的对抗面，现有方法难以同时覆盖。

Method: 提出模型无关的统一框架：(1) 结合范式特定防御机制与 GPBACC（隐私增强编码计算技术）；(2) 联邦学习中集成鲁棒聚合策略抵御恶意参与者；(3) 去中心化学习中采用近似解码比较和群组检测技术，实现轻量级验证与 adversary 隔离，无需可信聚合器。

Result: 通过攻击驱动分析，实施代表性隐私攻击和恶意行为攻击，实验表明 GPBACC 与鲁棒聚合及验证机制的组合显著降低了隐私泄露风险，并提升了对抗主动 adversary 的韧性。

Conclusion: 隐私增强编码计算与恰当的 adversary 抵抗策略相结合，可为安全分布式机器学习提供实用且可部署的基础方案。

Abstract: Distributed machine learning enables collaborative model training without centralizing data, but it also exposes learning processes to privacy leakage and malicious manipulation. Existing defenses typically address these threats in isolation and are often tailored to specific learning paradigms or model architectures, limiting their applicability in realistic deployments. In particular, federated learning and decentralized learning exhibit distinct adversarial surfaces that are rarely addressed within a unified framework. In this paper, we present a model-agnostic framework for adversary-resistant distributed learning that jointly addresses privacy preservation and malicious behavior across both federated and decentralized settings. Our approach combines paradigm-specific defense mechanisms with GPBACC, a privacy-enhancing coded computing technique applicable to arbitrary machine learning models. For federated learning, we integrate robust aggregation strategies to mitigate the impact of malicious participants, while for decentralized learning we employ approximate decode-and-compare and group testing techniques to enable lightweight verification and adversary isolation without relying on a trusted aggregator. Crucially, we evaluate the proposed framework through an explicit, attack-driven analysis. We implement representative privacy attacks and malicious behaviors, and empirically demonstrate that the combination of GPBACC with robust aggregation and verification mechanisms significantly reduces privacy leakage and improves resilience against active adversaries. These results suggest that privacy-enhancing coded computing, when combined with appropriate adversary-resistance strategies, provides a practical and deployable foundation for secure distributed machine learning.

</details>


### [180] [Online Resource Allocation with Continuous Random Consumption: Regret under Degeneracy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02196)
*Jiawei Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了连续奖励和消耗量分布下的在线资源分配问题，提出了一个基于加权质量指数的遗憾界理论，并设计了一种样本路径边际策略，实现了从O((log T)^2)到O(T^{1/2-1/(2p)})的遗憾界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的在线资源分配研究通常假设奖励或消耗量是离散的，或者依赖流体非退化假设。本文旨在处理连续分布场景，并允许流体松弛退化的情况（包括原始退化和对偶非唯一性），以填补这一理论空白。

Method: 提出了一个包含可观测类型、随机奖励和随机标量消耗量的在线资源分配模型。定义了活跃加权质量指数p来量化截止阈值附近请求的质量密度。设计了一种样本路径边际策略，并分别推导了p>1和p=1情况下的遗憾下界和上界。

Result: 当p>1时，任何在线策略都必须承受Ω(T^{1/2-1/(2p)})的遗憾，且样本路径边际策略匹配该下界（忽略多对数因子）；当p=1时，该策略实现O((log T)^2)的遗憾。例如，大小与价值-大小比独立均匀分布时p=1；大小与奖励独立均匀分布时p=2，策略在整个正则类中实现o(√T)的遗憾。

Conclusion: 该研究建立了一般连续分布下在线资源分配问题的遗憾界理论框架，无需流体非退化假设即可处理退化情况，并提供了严格匹配上下界的最优策略。

Abstract: We study online resource allocation when both rewards and consumption sizes may be continuously distributed. Requests arrive sequentially and must be accepted or rejected irrevocably under fixed resource capacities. Each request belongs to one of finitely many observable types; conditional on an observable request type, both the reward and the scalar size are random, and the realized size scales a fixed type-specific resource-consumption vector. The model allows the deterministic fluid relaxation to be degenerate.
  We show that additive regret is governed by the size-weighted mass of requests whose value-to-size ratios lie near the active acceptance cutoffs. We formalize this quantity through an active weighted-mass exponent p. When p > 1, this cutoff mass is thin, and the problem is genuinely hard: every online policy must incur regret of order at least $T^{1/2 - 1/(2p)}$, and this holds for every p > 1. A sample-path marginal policy matches this lower bound up to polylogarithmic factors; and when p = 1, so that the mass grows linearly near the cutoff, it attains $O((\log T)^2)$ regret. For example, if the size and the value-to-size ratio are independent and uniformly distributed, then p = 1; if instead the size and the reward are independent and uniformly distributed, then p = 2. Thus the policy achieves $o(\sqrt{T})$ regret throughout this regularity class without any fluid non-degeneracy assumption, allowing both primal degeneracy and dual non-uniqueness.

</details>


### [181] [Self-explainable Operator Learning for Discovering Spatial Patterns in Functional Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02203)
*Mojgan Alishiri,Amirhossein Arzani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种自解释的算子学习框架，通过将算子学习重构为积分方程表达的广义函数线性模型的线性组合，利用积分方程的可加分解性将输入域划分为子域并计算局部积分，从而直接实现模型的可解释性，无需外部工具即可揭示输入区域对输出的贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的算子学习方法基于神经网络架构，模型不透明，难以解释其预测背后的推理过程，限制了其在科学应用中的可信度。

Method: 将算子学习重构为积分方程表达的广义函数线性模型的线性组合，利用积分方程的可加分解性将输入域划分为子域并计算局部积分，从而将特定输入区域与对应输出模式直接关联。

Result: 在血流动力学和非定常空气动力学等流体问题上的函数到标量及函数到函数映射实验中，模型优先关注具有强特征梯度的区域，与事后可解释性方法定性一致，且可解释性内嵌于算子结构中。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了一种数学上透明、物理上可解释的方法来揭示数据内部关系，增强了机器学习在科学应用中的可信度，促进对物理系统进行更明智的数据驱动分析。

Abstract: Operator learning has emerged as a powerful tool for modeling complex physical systems in functional spaces. However, their neural network-based architectures make them opaque models, obscuring the reasoning behind their predictions. In this work, we introduce a self-explainable operator learning framework that overcomes this challenge by reformulating operator learning as a linear combination of generalized functional linear models expressed through integral equations. Exploiting the additive decomposability of these integral equations, we divide the input domain into subdomains and compute localized integrals to evaluate the contribution of each region to the final prediction. This decomposition enables direct interpretability where the model explains both inputs and outputs by linking specific input regions to corresponding output patterns, thereby revealing which spatial features drive predictions. We demonstrate the framework on function-to-scalar and function-to-function mappings in fluid flow problems involving blood flow and unsteady aerodynamics. The results show that the operator most often prioritizes regions with strong feature gradients, providing physically meaningful insight into the model's decision-making process. Comparisons with established post-hoc explainability methods demonstrate qualitative agreement while highlighting the key advantage of the proposed approach: explainability is embedded directly within the operator structure itself and does not require an external tool. Therefore, our framework provides a mathematically transparent and physically interpretable approach to uncover relationships within data, fostering trust in machine learning for scientific applications by enabling more informed data-driven analysis of physical systems.

</details>


### [182] [HERMES: A Multi-Granularity Labeling Substrate for Pre-training Data Mixtures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02266)
*Ziyun Qiao,Yue Min,Ruining Chen,Yujun Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HERMES通过学习语义变换加3阶段残差向量量化，为语料库构建可重用的层次化标签系统，替代传统固定粒度标签，支持从粗到细约13万个聚类单元的粒度控制，并通过1B参数、250亿token的预训练实验证明了层次化标签能揭示固定粒度流水线无法测试的交互效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据混合方法依赖预先划分的固定粒度标签系统（如来源、主题、格式分类或平面嵌入聚类），这些标签只能表达单一语义轴和单一粒度，改变分辨率需要重新构建标签，限制了混合器表达能力。作者认为瓶颈在于标签系统本身而非混合器。

Method: 提出HERMES标签底物：先对文档进行学习语义变换（Learned Semantic Transform），再通过3阶段残差向量量化（3-stage residual vector quantization）将每个文档编码为粗到细的层次化码，码的前缀长度控制粒度，支持最高约13万个聚类单元。

Result: 在粗粒度下，HERMES在标准聚类指标上与KMeans系列方法持平；在1B参数、250亿token的预训练实验中，层次结构暴露了固定粒度流水线无法测试的交互：在一个前缀长度下，组合Stage-2规则对比（等子桶覆盖 vs 桶内质量按比例top-30%）使16任务能力宏观平均提升+0.0253；在更细粒度下，候选池缩小约5倍后同一规则失去可测量优势。

Conclusion: HERMES将数据混合设计从在固定标签集之间选择重新定义为导航可重用的数据驱动粒度层次结构。

Abstract: Most data-mixing methods assume the corpus has already been partitioned into groups, and the choice of those groups determines what a mixer can express. Existing labels, including provenance, topic or format taxonomies, and flat embedding clusters, commit to one semantic axis at one granularity; changing the resolution rebuilds the labels. We argue the bottleneck is the label system, not the mixer, and provide a hierarchical one. HERMES is a data-derived labeling substrate: a Learned Semantic Transform followed by 3-stage residual vector quantization annotates each document once into a coarse-to-fine code whose prefix length controls granularity up to approximately 130k cells. At coarse granularity HERMES sits at a plateau with KMeans-family methods on standard clustering metrics, so the contribution is the substrate, not the clusterer. On 1B-parameter, 25B-token pre-training, the hierarchy exposes an interaction fixed-granularity pipelines cannot test: at one prefix length, a combined Stage-2 rule contrast, equal-subbucket coverage versus size-proportional within-bucket quality top-30%, lifts a 16-task capability macro-average by +0.0253; at the next finer level, the same rule loses its measurable edge as candidate pools contract approximately 5x. HERMES reframes data mixture design from choosing among fixed label sets to navigating a reusable, data-derived granularity hierarchy.

</details>


### [183] [Generalization in offline RL: The structure is more important than the amount of pessimism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02288)
*Max Weltevrede,Matthijs T. J. Spaan,Wendelin Böhmer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明在离线强化学习的上下文MDP中，过度悲观不会阻碍最优泛化，关键在于悲观结构是否尊重最优解的底层对称性。研究提出，数据增强应通过策略提取阶段的一致性损失来实现，而非传统的数据集增强训练，并在旋转对称的reacher环境中通过IQL和CQL实验验证了这一观点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究认为离线RL中的过度悲观会妨碍泛化，但本文试图论证悲观程度并非关键，真正影响泛化的是悲观结构是否与最优解的对称性一致。此外，本文希望重新审视数据增强在离线RL中的最佳应用方式。

Method: 理论证明：证明适度悲观的非对称值函数可能比过度悲观的对称值函数泛化更差。实践方法：提出将数据增强通过一致性损失应用于策略提取阶段，而非传统的数据集增强训练方式；在旋转对称reacher环境中使用IQL和CQL算法进行实证验证。

Result: 理论结果表明，悲观结构是否尊重最优解的对称性比悲观程度更重要；实验结果表明，在策略提取阶段使用一致性损失的数据增强方式优于传统的数据集增强训练方法。

Conclusion: 在离线RL的CMDPs中，过度悲观本身不会阻碍最优泛化；成功的泛化取决于悲观结构是否与最优解的对称性一致；数据增强的最佳应用方式是在策略提取阶段通过一致性损失实现，而非在常规离线训练中使用增强数据集。

Abstract: While pessimism counteracts overestimation bias in offline reinforcement learning (RL), being overly conservative has been associated with hindering certain forms of generalization. However, in this paper we demonstrate that being overly pessimistic does not inherently prevent optimal generalization in contextual MDPs (CMDPs). Instead, we argue successful generalization depends not on the amount of pessimism, but whether the pessimistic structure respects the underlying symmetries of the optimal solution. We prove that a mildly pessimistic, non-symmetric value function can generalize worse than an overly pessimistic, symmetric one. In offline RL, the structure of the pessimism is determined by the structure of the dataset coverage. As such, enforcing a symmetric value function can be non-trivial, and might require techniques such as data augmentation (DA). Inspired by our theoretical results, we argue that DA can best be applied through a consistency loss during policy extraction, rather than the common practice of (regular) offline training on an augmented dataset. This is empirically validated using IQL and CQL on a rotationally symmetric reacher environment.

</details>


### [184] [Optimizing Visual Generative Models via Distribution-wise Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02291)
*Ruihang Li,Mengde Xu,Shuyang Gu,Leigang Qu,Fuli Feng,Han Hu,Wenjie Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于分布奖励的微调框架，通过子集替换策略高效估计分布奖励，并采用强化学习优化模型合并系数，有效解决了传统逐样本奖励导致的奖励破解和模式崩溃问题，在SiT和EDM2等生成模型上显著提升了FID-50K指标和感知质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统强化学习策略在视觉生成中采用逐样本奖励函数，常导致奖励破解（reward hacking），造成图像多样性下降和视觉异常。现有方法忽略了样本间的分布关系，当所有样本独立向相同方向优化时易发生模式崩溃。

Method: 1) 提出分布级奖励（distribution-wise reward），评估样本整体数据分布而非单个样本；2) 引入子集替换策略（subset-replace strategy），仅更新生成参考集中的小子集以高效估计分布奖励；3) 应用RL优化后验模型合并系数（post-hoc model merging coefficients），缓解引入SDE带来的训练-推理不一致问题。

Result: SiT模型FID-50K从8.30降至5.77，EDM2模型从3.74降至3.52。定性评估证实方法提升了感知质量并保持了样本多样性。

Conclusion: 分布级奖励结合子集替换策略和RL模型合并优化，能有效缓解奖励破解和模式崩溃，在保持多样性的同时显著提升生成质量，为视觉生成模型的强化学习微调提供了新范式。

Abstract: Conventional reinforcement learning strategies for visual generation typically employ sample-wise reward functions, yet this practice frequently results in reward hacking that degrades image diversity and introduces visual anomalies. To address these limitations, we present a novel framework that finetunes generative models using distribution-wise rewards, ensuring better alignment with real-world data distributions. Unlike rewards that evaluate samples individually, distribution-wise reward accounts for the data distribution of the samples, mitigating the mode collapse problem that occurs when all samples optimize towards the same direction independently. To overcome the prohibitive computational cost of estimating these rewards, we introduce a subset-replace strategy that efficiently provides reward signals by updating only a small subset of a generated reference set. Additionally, we apply RL to optimize post-hoc model merging coefficients, potentially mitigating the train-inference inconsistency caused by introducing stochastic differential equation (SDE) in regular RL practices. Extensive experiments show our approach significantly improves FID-50K across various base models, from 8.30 to 5.77 for SiT and from 3.74 to 3.52 for EDM2. Qualitative evaluation also confirms that our method enhances perceptual quality while preserving sample diversity.

</details>


### [185] [One More Time: Revisiting Neural Quantum States from a Reinforcement Learning Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02292)
*Juan Agustín Duque,Sergio García Heredia,Vinicius Hernandes,Eliška Greplová,Thomas Spriggs,Aaron Courville,Anna Dawid*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Proximal Wavefunction Optimization (PWO)，一种基于信任域优化的神经量子态训练算法，通过裁剪概率比和相位增量来稳定变分能量最小化，在自旋系统和大规模RWKV-7模型上均优于Adam、minSR和SPRING等方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经量子态（NQS）中自回归模型虽能实现精确独立采样，但其优化策略尚不充分：Adam可扩展但忽略函数空间几何，随机重采样（SR）理论优美但计算昂贵且数值不稳定，亟需一种兼具可扩展性与理论保障的优化方法。

Method: 将变分能量最小化重新表述为Born分布上的优势策略梯度问题，引入信任域优化。PWO算法在振幅通道裁剪概率比变化，在相位通道裁剪相位增量，避免显式矩阵求逆，并支持样本复用多次更新。

Result: 在Ising和阻挫J1-J2自旋系统上，PWO在稳定性和收敛速度上优于Adam、minSR和SPRING；成功微调15亿参数的RWKV-7模型，将NQS优化规模提升三个数量级以上。

Conclusion: PWO融合了一阶优化的可扩展性与信任域方法的理论保证，为大规模神经量子态训练提供了一种实用且稳定的方案。

Abstract: Neural quantum states (NQS) provide a flexible and scalable framework for approximating quantum many-body wavefunctions. Among NQS parameterizations, autoregressive models are especially attractive because they enable exact, independent sampling from the Born distribution, avoiding the autocorrelation and mixing issues of Markov chain methods. Yet their optimization remains comparatively underexplored: Adam is a scalable method but ignores function space geometry, while stochastic reconfiguration is principled but costly and numerically fragile in large models. To address this gap, we show that variational energy minimization can be viewed as an advantage policy-gradient problem over the Born distribution, motivating trust-region optimization for NQS training. We introduce Proximal Wavefunction Optimization (PWO), a principled trust-region algorithm that clips probability-ratio changes in the amplitude channel and phase increments in the phase channel. PWO avoids explicit matrix inversion, reuses samples across multiple updates, and combines the scalability of first-order optimization with theoretical guarantees. Across Ising and frustrated $J_1$-$J_2$ one- and two-dimensional spin systems, PWO improves stability and wall-clock convergence over Adam, minSR, and SPRING. Finally, we fine-tune a $1.5$B-parameter RWKV-7 model, demonstrating NQS optimization at a scale over three orders of magnitude beyond prior work.

</details>


### [186] [Self-Gating Attention for Efficient Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02344)
*Dezheng Wang,Tong Chen,Wei Yuan,Congyan Chen,Shihua Li,Hongzhi Yin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Self-Gating Attention (SGA)，一种即插即用的线性复杂度注意力机制，通过共享可学习矩阵和输入相关残差组件替代标准自注意力中的QK计算，在保持竞争力的预测性能下显著提升推理效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准自注意力在时间序列预测中具有二次时间和内存复杂度（相对于回溯长度），限制了其在资源受限或高吞吐量场景下的使用。作者观察到时间序列自注意力图中存在跨时间步的冗余模式（源于重复的时间模式和相对稳定的时序相关性），因此希望设计一种更高效的注意力机制。

Method: 提出Self-Gating Attention (SGA)，将注意力分数表示为两部分之和：(1) 共享可学习矩阵，用于捕获常见的跨时间步注意力模式；(2) 输入相关残差分量，用于捕获输入特定的变化。SGA避免了标准注意力中的Query和Key投影计算，从而将时间和分数矩阵内存复杂度从二次降至线性。

Result: 在9个真实世界数据集（涵盖电力、金融、天气、医疗监测、人类活动和气候记录）上集成到多个预测主干中，SGA相比标准自注意力和轻量级注意力变体，在保持竞争力的预测性能（接近SOTA）的同时显著提升了推理效率。

Conclusion: SGA作为一种即插即用的轻量注意力机制，在线性复杂度下实现了与标准自注意力相当的预测性能，为时间序列预测中的高效部署提供了有力证据。

Abstract: Transformer architectures have shown strong potential in time series forecasting, where multi-head self-attention is widely used to capture temporal dependencies across historical timestamps. However, standard self-attention has quadratic time and memory complexity with respect to the look-back length. This cost may limit its use in resource-constrained or high-throughput forecasting systems, where fast and memory-efficient inference is important. Through qualitative and quantitative analyses, we observe that self-attention maps in time series forecasting often contain redundant patterns across different timestamps. This phenomenon can be related to the repeated temporal patterns and relatively stable temporal correlations in many real-world time series. Motivated by this observation, we propose Self-Gating Attention (SGA), a plug-and-play attention mechanism that represents the attention score with a shared learnable matrix and an input-dependent residual component. The shared matrix captures common attention patterns, while the residual component captures input-dependent variations. In this way, SGA avoids the query and key projections used in standard attention score computation, leading to linear time and score-matrix memory complexity with respect to the look-back length. We integrate SGA into several forecasting backbones and compare it with standard self-attention and lightweight attention variants on nine publicly available real-world datasets covering electricity, finance, weather, medical monitoring, human activity, and climate records. The results show that SGA improves inference efficiency on public benchmarks while maintaining competitive forecasting performance against state-of-the-art attention mechanisms. These benchmark results provide deployment-oriented evidence.

</details>


### [187] [DecompRL: Solving Harder Problems by Learning Modular Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02390)
*Juliette Decugis,Fabian Gloeckle,Francis Bach,Taco Cohen,Gabriel Synnaeve*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DecompRL算法，通过强化学习让LLM学会将复杂问题分解为可独立实现的子模块，并重组这些模块生成大量候选解，从而以更低的GPU成本解决标准方法无法解决的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法（重复采样和强化学习）在基础策略生成正确解的概率接近零时均失效，因为搜索空间过大无法通过更多采样或梯度信号克服。需要一种新策略来突破这一瓶颈。

Method: 提出DecompRL算法，采用强化学习显式训练模型学习分解问题并生成层次化代码结构。将问题分解为n个独立可解的子模块，每个模块有k个实现，重组后可获得k^n个候选解，将计算瓶颈从GPU推理转移到廉价的CPU评估。

Result: 在LiveCodeBench和CodeContests基准上（Qwen 2.5 7B和Code World Model 32B），DecompRL在每问题超过10^5 token时优于标准和多样性优化的RL基线，GPU token成本降低约50倍，能解决标准生成无法触及的问题。

Conclusion: 通过分解-重组策略，将难题转化为更小子问题的组合，可以显著扩展LLM的问题求解能力边界，是一种比单纯增加采样或优化策略更高效的方向。

Abstract: How can Large Language Models (LLMs) solve problems they currently cannot? Repeated sampling scales test-time compute but GPU cost grows linearly with attempts, while reinforcement learning (RL) with verifiable rewards improves single-attempt accuracy at the expense of sample diversity. Both strategies ultimately fail when the base policy has near-zero probability of producing a correct solution: no amount of sampling or gradient signal can overcome a search space that is simply too large. We take a different approach: rather than sampling harder, we make the task easier by decomposing problems into smaller, independently solvable sub-functions whose implementations can be recombined. Since off-the-shelf models are not trained for this modular generation, we introduce DecompRL, an RL algorithm that explicitly learns to decompose and implement hierarchical code structures. Recombining $k$ implementations of $n$ modules yields up to $k^{n}$ candidate solutions, shifting the bottleneck from GPU inference to cheap CPU evaluation and cutting GPU token cost by $\sim$50$\times$. On LiveCodeBench and CodeContests (Qwen~2.5~7B, Code World Model~32B), DecompRL outperforms standard and diversity-optimized RL baselines beyond $10^5$ tokens per problem, solving problems that standard generation cannot reach.

</details>


### [188] [Neuron-Aware Active Few-Shot Learning for LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02423)
*Zhuowei Chen,Liwei Chen,Christian Schunn,Raquel Coelho,Xiang Lorraine Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出NeuFS框架，利用神经元激活模式（而非输出层信号）进行主动少样本学习样本选择，通过双标准策略兼顾样本多样性与信息性，在推理和文本分类任务上超越现有AFSL方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有主动少样本学习方法依赖输出层信号（如预测熵、外部嵌入语义相似度）来选择样本，忽略了模型内部动态（如神经元激活模式），而这些内部信号能更精准地定位模型的知识盲区。

Method: 提出NeuFS框架，包含：(1) 直接使用神经元激活模式表示样本；(2) 双标准选择策略：基于神经元模式的多样性选择确保少样本覆盖广度，基于神经元共识的信息性选择优先识别LLM易产生幻觉的具有挑战性的样本。

Result: 在三个数据集上的实验表明，NeuFS在推理和文本分类任务中均优于现有AFSL基线方法。消融实验进一步证明内部神经元激活比外部嵌入提供更有效、更原则性的选择信号。

Conclusion: NeuFS验证了利用模型内部神经元动态进行主动少样本学习的优越性，为后续研究提供了从输出层转向内部信号的新范式。

Abstract: Active Few-Shot Learning (AFSL) adapts LLMs to specialized domains by identifying the most valuable unlabeled samples for annotation and use as few-shot demonstrations, effectively reducing human annotation costs while promoting high performance. However, existing methods typically rely on output-level signals for sample identification, such as predictive entropy or semantic similarities with test-time data based on external embeddings, which often overlook models' internal dynamics, which could pinpoint specific knowledge gaps. To bridge this gap, we propose NeuFS, a Neuron-Aware Active Few-Shot Learning framework that shifts the selection paradigm from output-level proxies to models' internal dynamics. NeuFS utilizes neuron activation patterns to represent sample directly, and includes a dual-criteria selection strategy that: (1) ensures few-shot sample diversity with neuron patterns for broader example coverage, while (2) prioritizing on identifying informative and challenging few-shot samples LLMs tend to hallucinate by quantifying neuron consensus. Experiments on three datasets demonstrate that NeuFS excels in both reasoning and text classification tasks, outperforming existing AFSL baselines. Ablation studies further highlight that internal neuron activations provide a more principled and effective selection signal than external embeddings, validating the superiority of the proposed NeuFS.

</details>


### [189] [QFedAgent: Quantum-Enhanced Personalized Federated Learning for Multi-Agent Activity Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02426)
*Quoc Bao Phan,Tuy Tan Nguyen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种混合量子-经典个性化联邦学习框架QFedAgent，用于多智能体活动识别。通过变分量子电路融合模块编码加速度计-陀螺仪交互，仅需72个量子旋转参数（对比经典MLP融合的33K参数），实现约10倍参数缩减，在OPPORTUNITY数据集上达到97.7%的平均测试准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体系统生成异构、非独立同分布的多模态传感器数据，导致传统联邦学习算法性能下降；经典融合模块引入大量参数开销和通信成本，亟需参数高效的融合方案。

Method: 提出QFedAgent框架：采用变分量子电路作为融合模块，通过量子态编码和纠缠机制建模加速度计与陀螺仪之间的交互关系，相比经典MLP融合大幅降低参数量（72 vs 33K），并结合个性化联邦学习应对非IID数据分布。

Result: 在OPPORTUNITY数据集基于主体非IID划分下，平均测试准确率达到97.7%，与经典联邦基线方法相比具有竞争力。

Conclusion: 参数高效的量子融合能够在保持竞争性能的同时显著降低模型参数量和通信开销，验证了量子-经典混合方法在联邦学习多模态传感任务中的可行性。

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) enables collaborative model training across distributed devices without sharing raw data, making it suitable for privacy-sensitive robotic sensing applications. However, multi-agent systems generate heterogeneous and non-independent and identically distributed (non-IID) multimodal sensor streams that degrade conventional FL algorithms, while classical fusion modules introduce substantial parameter overhead and communication cost. This paper proposes QFedAgent, a hybrid quantum-classical personalized FL framework for multi-agent activity recognition. The approach integrates a variational quantum circuit fusion module that models accelerometer--gyroscope interactions through quantum state encoding and entanglement, requiring only 72 quantum rotation parameters versus 33K in classical multi-layer perceptron-based fusion, achieving approximately 10x total parameter reduction. Experiments on the OPPORTUNITY dataset under subject-based non-IID partitions demonstrate 97.7% mean test accuracy, confirming that parameter-efficient quantum fusion remains competitive with conventional federated baselines.

</details>


### [190] [Extreme Adaptive Transformer for Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02437)
*Sanjeev Shrestha,Hui Liu,Yifan Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Exformer（极端自适应Transformer），通过引入包含Local、Stride和Extreme三个稀疏分量的极端自适应注意力机制，显式建模正常与极端事件之间的时序依赖关系，在四个真实水文径流数据集上实现了优于现有基线的3天预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间序列中的罕见极端事件（如洪水峰值）对预测至关重要，但现有Transformer模型对所有时间点均匀处理，难以充分捕捉极端模式。

Method: 提出Exformer框架，其核心是极端自适应注意力机制，包含三个稀疏分量：Local（捕获短期依赖）、Stride（捕获周期性依赖）和Extreme（选择性建模正常与极端事件之间的依赖关系）。

Result: 在四个真实水文径流数据集上，Exformer在3天预测任务中优于多种现有基线模型。

Conclusion: 显式引入极端感知注意力机制能够提升Transformer在不平衡时间序列（含罕见但重要事件）上的预测能力。

Abstract: Time series forecasting remains challenging when the underlying data contain rare but critical extreme events. This issue is particularly important in hydrologic forecasting, where streamflow distributions are often highly skewed and extreme peaks can have substantial impacts on flood monitoring, water resource management, and early warning systems. Although Transformer-based forecasting models have achieved strong performance by modeling long-range temporal dependencies, they typically treat all time points uniformly and may therefore underrepresent rare extreme patterns. In this paper, we propose the Extreme-Adaptive Transformer (Exformer), a forecasting framework designed to explicitly model temporal dependencies involving both normal and extreme events. Exformer introduces an extreme-adaptive attention mechanism composed of three sparse components: Local, Stride, and Extreme. The Local and Stride components capture short-term and periodic temporal dependencies, respectively, while the Extreme component selectively models event-aware dependencies between normal and extreme streamflow patterns. Experiments on four real-world hydrologic streamflow datasets show that Exformer achieves superior 3-day forecasting performance compared with state-of-the-art baselines. Our findings demonstrate that explicitly incorporating extreme-aware attention improves the forecasting capacity of Transformer models on imbalanced time series with rare but consequential events.

</details>


### [191] [Understanding the Robustness of Distributed Self-Supervised Learning Frameworks Against Non-IID Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02447)
*Xuanyu Chen,Nan Yang,Shuai Wang,Dong Yuan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对分布式自监督学习（D-SSL）在非独立同分布数据下的鲁棒性进行了严格理论分析，证明了掩码图像建模（MIM）比对比学习（CL）对异构数据更鲁棒，且联邦学习（FL）的鲁棒性不低于去中心化学习（DecL）。基于理论提出MAR损失函数，实验验证了理论发现及MAR损失的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式自监督学习（D-SSL）面临数据异构性的关键挑战，但现有研究缺乏对不同D-SSL框架在该挑战下鲁棒性的理论理解。

Method: 提出严格的数学理论分析框架，比较MIM和CL在非IID设置下的鲁棒性，分析网络连接性对鲁棒性的影响；基于理论设计MAR损失（带局部到全局对齐正则化的MIM目标改进）。

Result: 1) MIM预训练本质上比CL对异构数据更鲁棒；2) D-SSL的鲁棒性随平均网络连接性增加而提升，FL不比DecL鲁棒性差；3) MAR损失在实际实验中有效。

Conclusion: 理论分析为未来D-SSL算法设计提供了理论基础，并通过实验验证了理论和所提方法（MAR损失）的有效性。

Abstract: Recent research has introduced distributed self-supervised learning (D-SSL) approaches to leverage vast amounts of unlabeled decentralized data. However, D-SSL faces the critical challenge of data heterogeneity, and there is limited theoretical understanding of how different D-SSL frameworks respond to this challenge. To fill this gap, we present a rigorous theoretical analysis of the robustness of D-SSL frameworks under non-IID (non-independent and identically distributed) settings. Our results show that pre-training with Masked Image Modeling (MIM) is inherently more robust to heterogeneous data than Contrastive Learning (CL), and that the robustness of decentralized SSL increases with average network connectivity, implying that federated learning (FL) is no less robust than decentralized learning (DecL). These findings provide a solid theoretical foundation for guiding the design of future D-SSL algorithms. To further illustrate the practical implications of our theory, we introduce MAR loss, a refinement of the MIM objective with local-to-global alignment regularization. Extensive experiments across model architectures and distributed settings validate our theoretical insights, and additionally confirm the effectiveness of MAR loss as an application of our analysis.

</details>


### [192] [Neuron-Aware Data Selection for Annotation-Free LLM Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02460)
*Zhuowei Chen,Xiang Lorraine Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Neuron-OPSD框架，通过内部神经元激活引导训练数据选择和教师上下文构建，实现在无标注数据条件下的自蒸馏，提升领域内任务性能同时保持跨域泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有免标注自进化方法（如SFT/GRPO变体、基于奖励的on-policy RL）存在域外性能下降或校准误差膨胀问题，且专业领域人工标注成本高昂，需要一种无需真实交互反馈或人工标注的高效自蒸馏方法。

Method: 利用模型内部神经元激活值来指导训练数据选择和教师上下文构建，通过on-policy蒸馏从教师分布中训练模型，全程无需真实标签。

Result: 在专业领域基准上，Neuron-OPSD相比先前免标注基线方法，在提升领域内任务性能的同时保持了跨域泛化能力，并缓解了校准崩溃问题。

Conclusion: Neuron-OPSD是一种适用于在线交互或外部监督成本高昂场景的数据中心化框架，与依赖标注奖励轨迹的离线RL方法在概念上截然不同。

Abstract: Post-training large language models (LLMs) without real-world interaction feedback or human-labeled supervision remains challenging, particularly in specialized domains where expert annotations are costly to obtain. Recent annotation-free self-evolution methods address this by using the model's own outputs as supervision signals, constructing a teacher via additional context and aggregating predictions across multiple rollouts through majority voting to produce pseudo-labels. However, these approaches are not without drawbacks: SFT- and GRPO-based variants suffer out-of-domain performance degradation, while reward-based on-policy RL inflates calibration error. In this paper, we propose Neuron On-Policy Self-Distillation (Neuron-OPSD), a data-centric framework for annotation-free self-distillation that leverages internal neuron activations to guide both training-data selection and teacher context construction. The model is then trained via on-policy distillation from the teacher distribution, requiring no ground-truth labels at any stage. Across specialized-domain benchmarks, Neuron-OPSD improves in-domain task performance while preserving cross-domain generalization and mitigating calibration collapse over prior annotation-free baselines. This framework is particularly relevant to settings where online interaction or external supervision is costly or infeasible, and is conceptually distinct from offline RL approaches that rely on logged, reward-labeled trajectories.

</details>


### [193] [Beyond Adam: SOAP and Muon for Faster, Label-Efficient Training of Machine Learning Interatomic Potentials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02499)
*Gil Harari,Yoel Zimmermann,Ola Tangen Kulseng,Laura Zichi,Chuin Wei Tan,Marc L. Descoteaux,Boris Kozinsky*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了Muon、SOAP、SOAP-Muon等矩阵结构化优化器与Adam在训练NequIP和Allegro MLIP模型上的表现，发现SOAP和SOAP-Muon在收敛速度和最终精度上显著优于Adam，尤其在部分力监督条件下提升更为明显。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MLIP领域的研究长期聚焦于新架构和数据集，但训练优化器的选择却鲜有探索，社区默认使用Adam及其变体。本文旨在填补这一空白，系统评估矩阵结构化优化器在MLIP训练中的潜力。

Method: 在NequIP和Allegro两个MLIP模型上，实现并系统比较了Muon、SOAP、SOAP-Muon三类矩阵结构化优化器与Adam优化器的训练效果，涵盖收敛速度和最终精度等指标，并设置了部分力监督的实验条件。

Result: SOAP和SOAP-Muon在收敛速度和最终精度上均显著优于Adam；Muon仅带来部分提升。在部分力监督条件下，这些优化器的优势更为突出。

Conclusion: 优化器的选择是MLIP训练中一个被忽视但影响显著的设计维度，矩阵结构化优化器（尤其是SOAP和SOAP-Muon）有潜力成为替代Adam的更优选择。

Abstract: Machine learning interatomic potentials (MLIPs) have become a hallmark of AI for scientific simulation. While efforts on new architectures and datasets have led to increasingly accurate and general models, the choice of optimizer for training has largely remained unexplored, defaulting to Adam and its variants in the community. Here, we implement and systematically compare a class of recently proposed matrix-structured optimizers, including Muon, SOAP, and the hybrid SOAP-Muon, for training NequIP and Allegro MLIP models. We find that these optimizers can substantially outperform Adam in both convergence speed and final accuracy. SOAP and SOAP-Muon emerge as robust and consistently strong methods, while Muon only provides partial gains relative to Adam. The improvements are particularly pronounced under partial force supervision. Our results indicate that optimizer choice is an overlooked yet impactful design axis for MLIPs.

</details>


### [194] [DemoPSD: Disagreement-Modulated Policy Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02502)
*Yunhe Li,Hao Shi,Wenhao Liu,Mengzhe Ruan,Hanxu Hou,Zhongxiang Dai,Shuang Qiu,Linqi Song*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DemoPSD通过选择性采纳教师引导，构建反向KL重心目标，在保留学生自身推理能力的同时缓解特权信息泄露问题，在多个科学推理基准上优于GRPO和SDPO。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 同策略自蒸馏（OPSD）中教师基于特权信息的密集token级监督会导致过拟合、抑制探索、损害跨域泛化，并引发更根本的特权信息泄露问题——学生编码了测试时不可用的答案依赖捷径。

Method: 提出DemoPSD框架，通过构建教师分布与学生分布的反向KL重心目标（加权几何组合）作为优化目标，并利用分布差异自适应控制每个token位置的混合权重，实现选择性采纳教师引导。

Result: 在SciKnowEval四个科学领域上，DemoPSD优于GRPO和SDPO，保持更高训练熵，并稳健泛化到分布外GPQA基准。

Conclusion: DemoPSD在理论上实现了泄露衰减和探索保留，实践上取得了更优的推理性能与泛化能力。

Abstract: On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) has emerged as a practical method for training large language models (LLMs) to reason, where a single model acts as both the teacher and the student with different levels of information access. However, recent studies have found that the teacher's dense token-level supervision, conditioned on privileged information, can lead to overfitting to in-domain patterns, suppress exploration, and hurt cross-domain generalization, while also introducing a more fundamental issue: *privileged information leakage*, where the student encodes answer-dependent shortcuts that are unavailable at test time. We introduce **DemoPSD**, a novel framework that resolves such problems through the idea of *selective adoption of teacher guidance*. Instead of fitting the full teacher distribution, DemoPSD steers the student toward a *reverse-KL barycenter target*, a weighted geometric combination of the teacher and student distributions, that naturally balances learning from the teacher with preserving the student's own reasoning capacity. We measure the difference between their distributions and use such a discrepancy to adaptively control the blending at each token position. We provably show that DemoPSD achieves **(1)** *leakage attenuation*, i.e., effective mitigation of privileged information leakage; and **(2)** *exploration preservation*, i.e., preservation of exploration capacity under dense token-level distillation. Extensive experiments on SciKnowEval across four scientific fields show that DemoPSD outperforms both GRPO and SDPO while maintaining higher training entropy and robustly generalizing to out-of-distribution GPQA benchmarks.

</details>


### [195] [Program-as-Weights: A Programming Paradigm for Fuzzy Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02512)
*Wentao Zhang,Liliana Hotsko,Woojeong Kim,Pengyu Nie,Stuart Shieber,Yuntian Deng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"模糊函数编程"范式，通过PAW方法将自然语言规范编译为轻量级神经工件，使用4B编译器在10M数据集上训练，生成的参数高效适配器可在冻结的0.6B解释器上执行，以1/50的推理内存达到32B直接提示的性能，将大模型从逐问题求解器转变为工具构建器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 日常编程任务（如日志告警、JSON修复、搜索结果排序）难以用规则实现，日益依赖LLM API，但存在局部性差、不可重现、成本高的问题。本文旨在将自然语言规范编译为本地可执行的轻量级神经工件，克服上述局限。

Method: 提出模糊函数编程范式并实例化为PAW方法：一个4B编译器在FuzzyBench（1000万样本）上训练，为冻结的轻量级解释器（0.6B Qwen3）生成参数高效适配器，实现将自然语言规范编译为紧凑的本地可执行神经工件。

Result: PAW在0.6B解释器上执行时，匹配了直接提示Qwen3-32B的性能，推理内存仅为后者的约1/50，在MacBook M3上达到30 tokens/s的推理速度。

Conclusion: PAW将基础模型从逐输入问题求解器重新定义为工具构建器：每个函数定义只需编译一次，生成可复用的轻量级工件，后续函数应用调用成本低且可离线执行。

Abstract: Many everyday programming tasks resist clean rule-based implementation, such as alerting on important log lines, repairing malformed JSON, or ranking search results by intent, and are increasingly outsourced to large language model APIs at the cost of locality, reproducibility, and price. We propose fuzzy-function programming: compiling such a function from a natural-language specification into a compact, locally-executable neural artifact. We instantiate this paradigm with Program-as-Weights (PAW), in which a 4B compiler trained on FuzzyBench, a 10M-example dataset we release, emits parameter-efficient adapters for a frozen, lightweight interpreter. A 0.6B Qwen3 interpreter executing PAW programs matches the performance of direct prompting of Qwen3-32B, while using roughly one fiftieth of the inference memory and running at 30 tokens/s on a MacBook M3. PAW reframes the foundation model from a per-input problem solver into a tool builder: invoked once per function definition, it produces a small reusable artifact whose subsequent calls per function application are cheap and offline.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [196] [Benchmarking Code Improvement with Progressive, Adaptive, and Interactive Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01360)
*Cuong Chi Le,Aashish Yadavally,Minh Le-Anh,Tien N. Nguyen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: PAIR-Bench 是一个渐进式自适应基准，用于评估大语言模型通过反馈引导改进代码的能力，采用渐进式提示（progressive hinting）协议，通过失败区域控制和提示深度控制两个维度，对模型修复轨迹进行细粒度评估，超越传统的二元功能正确性判断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 评估通常基于二元功能正确性（通过/不通过测试套件），忽略了部分进展、反馈利用、回归问题以及模型改进代码的细化轨迹，缺乏对代码改进过程的细粒度衡量。

Method: 提出 PAIR-Bench，采用渐进式提示（progressive hinting）协议，包含两个控制维度：失败区域控制（failure-region control）决定反馈目标，通过将隐藏的失败测试分组为失败场景；提示深度控制（hint-depth control）决定揭示多少修复相关信息，从粗略症状到实现级指导。

Result: PAIR-Bench 能够衡量模型是否修复了目标失败、是否能在提示之外泛化、是否保持已有正确行为，以及需要多少辅助。通过评估修复轨迹而非仅最终通过/失败结果，提供了更细粒度的 LLM 代码改进能力评估。

Conclusion: PAIR-Bench 通过渐进式反馈协议实现了对 LLM 代码改进能力的多维细粒度评估，弥补了传统二元评估的不足。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are typically evaluated on code generation and program repair using binary functional correctness: a generated program or patch either passes or fails a test suite. This protocol is simple but coarse, as it ignores partial progress, feedback use, regressions, and the refinement trajectory through which models often improve code. We introduce PAIR-Bench, a progressive and adaptive benchmark for evaluating code improvement: transforming an incorrect or incomplete program into a more correct one through feedback-guided refinement. PAIR-Bench uses progressive hinting, a structured feedback protocol with two controls. Failure-region control determines what the feedback targets by grouping hidden failing tests into failure scenarios, while hint-depth control determines how much repair-relevant information is revealed, from coarse symptoms to implementation-level guidance. This design enables PAIR-Bench to measure whether a model repairs targeted failures, generalizes beyond the hint, preserves already-correct behavior, and how much assistance it requires. By evaluating repair trajectories progressive metrics rather than only final pass/fail outcomes, PAIR-Bench provides a finer-grained assessment of LLM code-improvement capability.

</details>


### [197] [A global predicted-fMRI drive signal from TRIBE does not predict YouTube replay heatmaps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01400)
*Barada Sahu,Shivesh Pandey*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文测试了2025年Algonauts脑编码挑战赛冠军模型TRIBE（基于Llama-3.2、V-JEPA2和Wav2Vec-BERT）预测的神经信号是否能预测视频观看行为。结果发现，该模型预测的皮层响应与YouTube"最常重播"热力图之间的相关性接近于零（部分相关+0.058，p=0.23），且不优于简单的响度和运动基线。该无效结果在多种皮层网络读数和自相关保持置换检验下均成立，音乐视频中观察到的中等相关被归因为类型特定的开头/重播伪影，不具有泛化性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究深度多模态脑编码模型预测的神经信号能否进一步预测自然刺激下的行为参与度（即视频重播行为），评估脑编码模型的行为预测效度。

Method: 使用TRIBE模型处理48个YouTube视频，将预测的皮层响应通过全局场功率降维为每秒参与度曲线，与YouTube"最常重播"热力图进行位置控制的部分相关分析，并与响度和运动基线比较，同时进行六种皮层网络读出的敏感性分析和自相关保持置换检验。

Result: 预测曲线与重播行为之间的部分相关为+0.058（95% CI [-0.04, 0.15]），不显著（p=0.23），且不优于响度（+0.04，p=0.74）和运动基线。音乐视频中的中等相关被证明是开头/重播伪影而非内容预测。

Conclusion: 当前最先进的脑编码模型预测的神经信号不能预测视频观看行为，表明脑编码精度与行为预测之间可能存在差距，需要进一步研究如何桥接神经预测与行为预测。

Abstract: Deep multimodal brain-encoding models now predict fMRI responses to naturalistic video with high accuracy. Whether their predicted neural signals also forecast behavioral engagement is unknown. We run TRIBE, the winning model of the 2025 Algonauts brain-encoding challenge (Llama-3.2 + V-JEPA2 + Wav2Vec-BERT), on 48 YouTube videos and reduce its predicted cortical response to a per-second engagement curve, the global field power. Correlated against each video's "most replayed" heatmap, a passively-collected proxy for which moments viewers return to, the curve shows no evidence of predicting re-watch behavior. The pooled position-controlled partial correlation is +0.058 (95% CI [-0.04, 0.15]; one-sample t(47)=1.21, p=0.23), indistinguishable from zero and not significantly above simple loudness and motion baselines (loudness +0.04, paired p=0.74). The raw correlation is also near zero; the moderate values reported for music videos reflect a genre-specific intro/onset-replay artifact rather than content prediction, and do not generalize. The null holds across six cortical-network readouts and under an autocorrelation-preserving permutation test. We release the code, the video-ID manifest, and an acquisition method that works despite YouTube's SABR-only streaming.

</details>


### [198] [GPUAlert: A Zero-Instrumentation Process-Boundary Monitor for Diagnosing GPU Training-Job Failures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01409)
*Parv Agarwal,Asif Ekbal*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: GPUAlert是一个命令行包装器，可在无需修改训练脚本的情况下监控GPU训练任务，通过三项可靠性原语（预启动日志保证、通知器隔离、非静默工件预算）实现失败原因分类、持久化日志和工件保存，并能在SMTP不可达时保持子进程退出码不变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GPU训练任务在大规模生产集群中失败率高（约40%），但运维人员通常数小时后才能得知失败，现有方案（实验追踪器需修改脚本并保持云端连接，调度器邮件钩子只返回单行状态而无原因和日志）均无法有效解决此问题。

Method: 设计了三项可靠性原语：1）预启动日志保证——在子进程可能崩溃前建立持久化日志目标；2）通知器隔离——使包装器的退出码仅由子进程状态决定，不依赖邮件是否发送成功；3）非静默工件预算——在有限附件大小内不静默丢弃输出。基于有序规则分类器实现失败原因分类。

Result: 在12个硬件复现的失败类别上，有序规则分类器macro-F1达0.997，优于无序关键词匹配（0.830）和退出码检查（0.133）；包装器开销恒定约3ms/任务；预启动保证在shell重定向无输出的情况下仍能保留日志；所有15种失败模式下均能保持子进程退出码不变。

Conclusion: GPUAlert通过进程边界的无侵入式监控和精心设计的可靠性原语，实现了零修改、高精度的训练失败检测与通知，显著优于现有的关键词匹配和退出码检查方法。

Abstract: GPU training jobs fail often, roughly two in five on large production clusters, yet the operator typically learns of a failure only by reconnecting hours later. Experiment trackers require editing the training script and maintaining a cloud connection; the scheduler's mail hook delivers a single status line with no cause and no logs. GPUAlert is a command-line wrapper that monitors any training command at the process boundary, and with no change to that command, emails a structured notification on completion carrying a classified failure cause, durable logs, and output artifacts. The tool is organized around three reliability primitives: a pre-launch log guarantee that establishes the durable destination before the child process can crash, notifier isolation that makes the wrapper's exit code a pure function of the child's status regardless of whether the email succeeds, and a non-silent artifact budget that bounds attachment size without ever dropping output silently. We release a labelled corpus of 474 GPU training logs across 15 failure classes and a reproducible evaluation harness. On the twelve hardware-reproduced classes, the ordered-rule classifier reaches 0.997 macro-F1, against 0.830 for unordered keyword matching and 0.133 for exit-code inspection. Wrapper overhead is a constant approximately 3ms per job; the pre-launch guarantee preserves a log where a shell redirect yields nothing; and across all 15 failure modes the wrapper returns the child's exit code unchanged even when the SMTP relay is unreachable.

</details>


### [199] [Adoption and Impact of Command-Line AI Coding Agents: A Study of Microsoft's Early 2026 Rollout of Claude Code and GitHub Copilot CLI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01418)
*Emerson Murphy-Hill,Jenna Butler,Alexandra Savelieva*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 通过对微软2026年初数万名工程师的CLI编码代理工具（如Claude Code、Copilot CLI）推广研究，发现：首次使用主要通过社交网络传播；留存率与编码活跃度相关而非人口统计学特征；采用者合并PR数量比基线增加约24%。结果表明这些工具并非均匀采用或纯新奇效应，组织应将可见的同伴使用作为推广策略核心。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组织大规模部署代理型命令行工具（如Claude Code、Copilot CLI）时面临成本高昂（年Token支出可达数百万美元）且缺乏科学依据的决策困境，需要了解谁会试用、谁会持续使用、工具产出是否值得成本。

Method: 对微软2026年初推广期间数万名工程师的使用数据进行分析，追踪首次使用传播、留存率影响因素，并以合并PR数量作为产出代理指标衡量采用者与基线对比的提升幅度，观察期跨四个月。

Result: 首次使用主要通过社交网络传播；留存率与工程师编码活动水平相关而非人口统计特征；采用者合并PR数量比基线提高约24%，且该提升在四个月观察窗口内持续存在。

Conclusion: CLI编码代理工具既非均匀采用也非纯粹新奇效应，组织应将可见的同伴使用作为推广策略的核心要素。

Abstract: Organizations rolling out agentic command line tools like Anthropic's Claude Code and GitHub's Copilot CLI need to know who will try them, who will keep using them, and whether the tools produce enough output to justify their cost. At organizational scale, token spend can run into millions of dollars annually, so misreading adoption, retention, or impact can make a rollout expensive without changing engineering velocity. Studying tens of thousands of engineers at Microsoft over its early-2026 rollout, we find that first use spread primarily through social networks, retention was associated more with engineers' coding activity than with demographics, and adopters merged roughly 24% more pull requests than they would have otherwise. We use merged pull requests as our proxy for output -- acknowledging that a merged PR is not the same as the value it delivers -- and the lift persists across our four-month window. These results suggest that CLI coding agents are neither uniformly adopted nor mere novelty effects and that organizations should treat visible peer use as central to rollout strategy.

</details>


### [200] [Risk Architecture for AI-Native Engineering Teams: An Organizational Framework for Agentic System Governance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01421)
*Laxmipriya Ganesh Iyer*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文发现现有软件风险管理框架（基于确定性、可审计变更和清晰组件映射）不适用于AI Agent系统。作者提出七维团队画像、六类故障模式（含新发现的"依赖边界确定性失配"），以及框架充分性评估方法。研究发现：从纯软件工程到AI原生团队，风险覆盖能力单调下降，最严重的未覆盖故障出现在AI原生团队的输出被确定性依赖消费的组织边界处。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有软件工程风险管理框架（责任归属、严重性分级、测试覆盖）隐含假设系统行为确定、变更事件离散可审计、组件-所有者映射清晰，但构建和运营AI Agent系统的团队同时违反这三项假设：输出概率性、系统自主执行多步操作、风险面在部署间静默变化。现有AI风险文献要么从上层（政策框架如NIST AI RMF、ISO/IEC 42001）要么从下层（威胁分类如OWASP的Agentic AI指南）处理问题，但未覆盖工程管理者的操作层——角色、决策权和上报结构。

Method: 本文贡献了三个方法论成果：(i) 七维画像——区分纯软件工程、混合型和AI原生团队；(ii) 六类故障模式分类法——包含此前未被阐述的"依赖边界确定性失配"类别；(iii) 合成框架充分性方法论——评分每种画像的风险架构在检测、遏制和上报定义场景集时的表现。评估基于框架充分性而非人类行为，因此产生的是推导性而非观察性的覆盖率声明。

Result: 覆盖率随团队从纯软件工程向AI原生运营转变而下降：中位数呈单调下降，且仅在AI原生步骤中出现大量未被覆盖的高后果故障。退化集中在特定故障模式类别。最严重、覆盖最差的故障并非出现在AI原生团队内部，而是在其概率输出被确定性假设依赖消耗的组织边界处。

Conclusion: 现有风险管理框架在AI Agent团队中失效，且最关键的缺口存在于AI系统与其确定性消费者之间的接口处，而非AI系统内部。这提示工程管理实践需要新的风险架构来应对概率性输出带来的跨界故障。

Abstract: Engineering management research has produced mature frameworks for software risk: ownership by feature, escalation by severity, and assurance by test coverage. These frameworks implicitly assume deterministic behavior, discrete and auditable change events, and clear component-to-owner mappings. Teams that build and operate agentic AI systems violate all three assumptions at once: outputs are probabilistic, systems take autonomous multi-step actions, and the risk surface mutates silently between deployments. Existing AI risk literature addresses this from above (policy frameworks such as the NIST AI RMF and ISO/IEC 42001) or below (threat taxonomies such as OWASP's agentic AI guidance), but not at the layer where an engineering manager (EM) operates: roles, decision rights, and escalation structures. This paper contributes (i) a seven-dimension profile distinguishing pure software-engineering, hybrid, and AI-native teams; (ii) a six-cluster failure-mode taxonomy including a previously unarticulated cluster, dependency-boundary determinism mismatch; and (iii) a synthetic framework-adequacy methodology scoring how well each profile's risk architecture detects, contains, and escalates a defined scenario set. Because the object of study is framework adequacy rather than human behavior, the evaluation yields derived rather than observed coverage claims. Coverage degrades as teams move from pure software engineering to AI-native operation, monotonically in the median and abruptly in the count of uncovered, high-consequence failures appearing only at the AI-native step. The degradation concentrates in specific failure-mode categories, and the most severe, least-covered failures arise not inside AI-native teams but at the organizational boundary where their probabilistic outputs are consumed by determinism-assuming dependencies.

</details>


### [201] [From Anatomy to Smells: An Empirical Study of SKILL.md in Agent Skills](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01456)
*David Boram Hong,Aaron Imani,Iftekhar Ahmed*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 对238个真实世界的Agent Skill文件（SKILL.md）进行首次系统研究，通过定性分析归纳出13个高层级和44个低层级的语义组件分类体系，通过多声部文献综述识别最佳实践并定义技能坏味，开发自动化检测工具发现超过99%的文件存在至少一种坏味且难以消除，揭示了实际编写实践与推荐实践之间的显著差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Agent Skills被广泛采用，但SKILL.md文件的编写方式以及现有编写指南是否被遵循尚不清楚，缺乏对这一软件制品的系统研究。

Method: 定性分析238个真实技能文件归纳语义分类体系；对29篇文献进行多声部文献综述提取最佳实践并定义技能坏味；开发自动化检测工具并对真实技能文件进行检测和演化分析。

Result: 归纳出13个高层级和44个低层级语义组件；定义了一系列技能坏味；自动化检测发现超过99%的SKILL.md文件包含至少一个技能坏味；一旦引入，坏味在技能演化过程中很少消失。

Conclusion: 实际编写实践与推荐实践之间存在显著差距，需要开发自动化技术来修复技能坏味，同时提高开发者对这一新兴质量问题的认识。

Abstract: Agent Skills provide on-demand domain knowledge to LLM agents without requiring model retraining. Each Agent Skill is defined by a mandatory SKILL.md file containing metadata and an unstructured Markdown body whose contents are left entirely to the skill author. Despite the rapid adoption of Agent Skills, little is known about how these files are authored or whether existing authoring guidelines are followed in practice. In this paper, we present the first systematic study of SKILL.md files as a software artifact. We qualitatively analyze 238 real-world skills and derive a taxonomy of 13 higher-level and 44 lower-level semantic components. We then conduct a multivocal literature review of 29 sources to identify best practices for authoring SKILL.md files and introduce skill smells as violations of these practices. Finally, we develop an automated detector and apply it to real-world skills, finding that over 99% of SKILL.md files contain at least one skill smell, and once introduced, skill smells rarely disappear as skills evolve. These findings reveal a substantial gap between recommended and actual authoring practices, motivating the development of automated techniques to remediate skill smells while increasing developer awareness of this emerging quality issue.

</details>


### [202] [Insights from GitHub Community on the Matter Standard: Developer Perspectives and Challenges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01494)
*Muhammad Hassan,Carl Gunter,Susan Landau,Masooda Bashir*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过分析Project CHIP GitHub仓库中超过13,000个issue，研究了开发者在实现和集成Matter物联网标准时遇到的实际问题。通过主题建模和定性分析，识别出测试、互操作性、开发、平台与网络四个主要问题领域，并描述了它们在代码库和工具演进中的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Matter标准旨在解决物联网中的长期互操作性问题，但开发者日常使用该标准时的实际体验尚不明确，缺乏系统性研究。

Method: 对Project CHIP GitHub仓库中超过13,000个issue进行主题建模和定性分析。

Result: 识别出四个反复出现的关注领域：测试、互操作性、开发、平台与网络，并揭示了这些领域在代码库和工具演进中的具体表现。

Conclusion: 研究发现了系统性的技术和集成挑战，并指出了改进Matter测试基础设施、跨供应商指南和文档的具体机会，以帮助标准继续成熟。

Abstract: Matter seeks to resolve longstanding interoperability problems in the Internet of Things (IoT), yet little is known about how developers experience the standard in day to day work. This paper examines over 13,000 issues from the official Project CHIP GitHub repository to understand the kinds of problems contributors report when implementing and integrating Matter. Using topic modeling and qualitative analysis, we identify four recurring areas of concern, Testing, Interoperability, Development, and Platform and Network, and describe how they manifest in the evolution of the codebase and tooling. The findings reveal systematic technical and integration challenges and point to concrete opportunities to refine Matter's test infrastructure, cross vendor guidance, and documentation as the standard continues to mature.

</details>


### [203] [Kani: A Model Checker for Rust](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01504)
*Rémi Delmas,Zyad Hassan,Qinheping Hu,Rahul Kumar,Felipe R. Monteiro,Thanh Nguyen,Adrián Palacios,Celina Val,Michael Tautschnig,Justus Adam,Daniel Schwartz-Narbonne,Carolyn Zech*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Kani是一个基于有界模型检查（BMC）的开源验证工具，通过将Rust的MIR编译到CBMC引擎，自动检查不安全操作、功能正确性和运行时panic等属性，并支持函数契约、循环契约、量词和函数桩等规范语言实现从有界到无界的验证扩展，已在工业Rust项目（含标准库）中发现6个未知bug并实现超16,000个测试的CI级验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Rust的ownership类型系统能在safe代码中防止内存错误，但unsafe操作的可靠性、功能正确性和无运行时panic等关键属性仍缺乏编译期保障，亟需一种能形式化验证这些属性的方法。

Method: 将Rust的MIR编译到CBMC的位精确验证引擎，自动检查全套安全属性；通过函数契约、循环契约、量词和函数桩等规范语言将有界验证扩展到无界验证。

Result: 在工业Rust项目中验证了可行性，通过契约将验证从panic-free升级到功能正确性，发现6个未知bug；在Rust标准库验证活动中实现每代码变更超16,000个测试的CI规模验证。

Conclusion: Kani证明了有界模型检查可以超越bug查找，为Rust的核心性质提供正确性保证，且能在生产CI环境中大规模运行。

Abstract: Rust's ownership type system prevents memory errors in safe code, but certain desirable properties remain orthogonal to compilation: the soundness of unsafe operations (e.g., raw pointer dereferences), functional correctness, and absence of runtime panics. We present Kani, an open-source model checker for Rust that pushes bounded model checking beyond bug-finding to provide correctness guarantees for these properties. Kani compiles proof harnesses from Rust's Mid-level Intermediate Representation (MIR) into CBMC's bit-precise verification engine, automatically checking a comprehensive set of safety properties with no user annotation. To extend verification from bounded to unbounded, Kani provides a specification language comprising function contracts, loop contracts, quantifiers, and function stubbing. We demonstrate feasibility through case studies on industrial Rust projects, where contracts upgraded verification from panic-freedom to functional correctness, uncovering six previously unknown bugs. Kani operates at scale in production CI, with over 16,000 harnesses verified per code change in the Rust standard library verification campaign.

</details>


### [204] [A Capacity-Aware Parr Model for Agile Projects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01562)
*Pedro E. Colla*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文针对传统软件努力分布模型（如PNR家族和Parr替代曲线）在敏捷环境中因团队容量固定或受限而难以直接应用的问题，提出了Parr模型的重构版本，将其作为容量感知的敏捷项目预测层。该模型将归一化的Parr形状潜在努力需求与观察或计划的容量轨迹相结合，可预测总体进度、完成时间、容量赤字和容量冗余，且不假设资源限制下遵循相同的内部活动路径。模型参数集精简，包括总工作量K、Parr形状参数、可匹配非零初始人员的原点常数c以及容量轨迹。提供了离散冲刺公式、从普通Scrum记录中校准的方法以及滚动原点验证协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统努力分布模型（PNR家族和Parr曲线）隐含了特定的人员配置模式，在敏捷环境中团队容量固定、部分固定或受外部约束时，原始曲线可能规定组织无法分配的人员需求，导致其直接使用受限。因此需要一种容量感知的预测方法。

Method: 对Parr模型进行紧凑重构：将归一化的Parr形状潜在努力需求与观测或计划的容量轨迹相结合，构建容量感知预测层。采用小参数集（总工作量K、Parr形状参数、原点常数c、容量轨迹），提供离散冲刺公式、基于普通Scrum记录的校准方法以及滚动原点验证协议。

Result: 模型可预测总体进度、完成时间、容量赤字和容量冗余，且不假设资源限制下遵循相同的内部活动路径。通过滚动原点验证协议与简单管理基线进行比较评估。

Conclusion: 该模型为敏捷项目提供了容量感知的预测框架，比完整因果理论范围更窄但实用性强，可在团队容量约束下有效预测项目进展。

Abstract: Classical software effort distribution models, including the PNR family and Parr alter native curve, were designed to describe the time distribution of development effort under an implied staffing pattern. Their direct use in agile environments is limited when team capacity is fixed, partially fixed, or externally constrained, the original curve may prescribe a staff demand that the organization cannot allocate. This paper proposes a compact refactoring of Parr model as a capacity-aware forecasting layer for agile projects. The contribution is deliberately narrower than a full causal theory of project dynamics. A normalized Parr shaped latent effort demand is combined with an observed or planned capacity trajectory. The resulting model forecasts aggregate progress, completion time, capacity deficit, and capacity slack without assuming that the same internal activity path is followed under resource restriction. The model uses a small parameter set such as total effort K, a Parr shape parameter, an origin constant c that can match nonzero initial staffing, and the capacity trajectory. A discrete sprint formulation is provided, together with a calibration method from ordinary Scrum records and a rolling origin validation protocol against simple management baselines.

</details>


### [205] [A Single Patch Is Not Enough: Deterministic Fusion of Repair Candidates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01597)
*Boyang Yang,Xiangliang Hu,Luyao Ren,Yanjun Chen,Bach Le,Tegawendé F. Bissyandé,Haoye Tian*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: PatchFusion提出了一种确定性的原子证据融合方法，用于在无需测试结果的情况下，从候选补丁池中选择并构建最终补丁，在三个基准上超越了所有匹配的候选池选择器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM编码智能体常用pass@k评估，但实际开发中仅应用单个补丁。现有后处理方法（排序、测试过滤、LLM评判）均未利用共享编辑原子证据来同时选择和构建最终补丁，存在pass@k到pass@1的差距。

Method: PatchFusion首先将全差异一致性融合到修复邻域，选择一个可审计的代表性补丁，然后应用证据约束融合(ECF)保留重复编辑原子并剪枝无支持部分。同时构建了PatchFuseBench固定池基准。

Result: 在SWE-bench Verified解决426/500个bug，SWE-bench Multilingual解决236/300个bug，Defects4J达到87/371个合理补丁。恢复41个和27个任何单一来源未解决的bug。ECF分别增加+5/+6/+9个已解决bug。达到可达上限的96.2%和89.7%。

Conclusion: 跨候选证据融合比基于测试和LLM的选择器能恢复更多正确补丁，且成本低几个数量级，无观察到的回归现象。

Abstract: Modern LLM coding agents are commonly evaluated using pass@k, but developers typically apply a single final patch in real-world settings. This pass@k-to-pass@1 gap is a post-generation problem: a candidate patch pool may contain a correct patch, but the system must decide which one to suggest to developers. Existing post-generation approaches mainly rank whole candidates, filter them with tests, or query an LLM judge, but none deterministically reuse shared edit-atom evidence to both select and construct the final patch. Thus, we propose PatchFusion, a deterministic atomic evidence fusion approach for candidate patches that consults no test outcome at decision time. PatchFusion first fuses whole-diff agreement into a repair neighborhood, selects an auditable representative, and then applies evidence-constrained fusion (ECF) to retain repeated edit atoms and prune unsupported parts. To evaluate this setting, we build PatchFuseBench, a fixed-pool benchmark covering SWE-bench Verified, SWE-bench Multilingual, and Defects4J candidate patches.
  On PatchFuseBench, PatchFusion solves 426/500 bugs on SWE-bench Verified and 236/300 on SWE-bench Multilingual, and reaches 87/371 plausible patches on Defects4J, outperforming every matched candidate-pool selector on all three. PatchFusion recovers 41 and 27 bugs that no single source solves (30 and 18 more over the best single source). Ablation studies show that ECF adds +5/+6/+9 solved bugs by recovering in-pool repairs that selection misses, with no observed regression, and that PatchFusion's gains remain stable as candidate pools are resampled. On these complementary multi-source pools, cross-candidate evidence recovers more correct patches than the test-based and LLM-based selectors we evaluate, at orders-of-magnitude lower cost, reaching within 96.2% and 89.7% of the candidate-reachable ceiling on the two SWE-bench benchmarks.

</details>


### [206] [AgentFlow: Building Agent Dependency Graphs for Static Analysis of Agent Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01640)
*Shenao Wang,Xinyi Hou,Yanjie Zhao,Xiao Cheng,Haoyu Wang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: AgentFlow 是首个用于从智能体程序中恢复和分析智能体依赖关系的静态分析框架，通过构建智能体依赖图（ADG）实现，在 5,399 个真实智能体程序上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 智能体程序结合了传统代码和框架定义的语义（模型、提示、工具、记忆、多智能体编排），其行为依赖于新型的"智能体依赖关系"，现有静态分析或依赖追踪工具难以恢复这些依赖。

Method: 提出 AgentFlow，构建智能体依赖图（ADG）——一种框架无关的图表示，将智能体、提示、模型、能力、记忆状态和控制策略作为类型化节点，将组件依赖、控制流和数据流依赖作为类型化边。在五个代表性智能体框架上实现，并在 5,399 个真实程序组成的 AgentZoo 语料库上评估。

Result: AgentFlow 恢复的智能体实体和依赖关系比现有基于 AST 的静态分析工具更丰富；生成的智能体物料清单（BOM）更具依赖感知能力；在真实智能体程序中发现了 238 个污点风格的提示到工具风险。

Conclusion: ADG 为理解、治理和保障新兴智能体软件提供了实用的基础。

Abstract: LLM agents are increasingly developed as source-code applications built on agent frameworks. These agent programs combine conventional host-language code with framework-defined semantics for models, prompts, tools, memory, and multi-agent orchestration logic. As a result, their behavior depends not only on traditional control and data flows, but also on a new class of agent dependencies. Such dependencies are often expressed as framework-induced semantics, such as agent constructors, tool decorators, and agent handoff declarations, making them difficult to recover with existing static analysis or dependency tracking tools. In this paper, we present AgentFlow, the first static analysis framework for recovering and analyzing agent dependencies from agent programs. AgentFlow constructs an Agent Dependency Graph (ADG), a framework-agnostic graph representation that represents agents, prompts, models, capabilities, memory states, and control policies as typed nodes, and captures their component-dependency, control-flow, and data-flow dependencies as typed edges. Built on ADGs, AgentFlow supports a range of analyses for agent governance and security, including Agent Bill of Materials (BOM) generation and prompt-to-tool risk detection. We implement AgentFlow for five representative agent frameworks and evaluate it on AgentZoo, a corpus of 5,399 real-world agent programs. Our evaluation shows that AgentFlow recovers richer agent entities and dependencies than existing AST-based agent static analysis tools, generates more dependency-aware Agent BOMs, and uncovers 238 taint-style prompt-to-tool risks in real-world agent programs. These results show that ADG provides a practical foundation for understanding, governing, and securing emerging agent software.

</details>


### [207] [When Agents Do Not Stop: Uncovering Infinite Agentic Loops in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01641)
*Xinyi Hou,Shenao Wang,Yanjie Zhao,Haoyu Wang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对LLM智能体中无限智能体循环(IAL)问题的静态分析工具IAL-Scan。该工具将异构智能体代码抽象为统一的Agent IR，构建Agentic Loop Dependence Graph来识别反馈路径，并检测可能无限循环的代价高昂或状态增长操作。在6,549个仓库的评估中，IAL-Scan以91.9%的精度发现了68个实际IAL故障。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体在迭代执行中会出现一种新型故障：当反馈路径未被有效约束时，智能体会无限重复执行模型调用、工具调用、工作流转换或智能体切换，导致成本耗尽、模型拒绝服务、上下文膨胀等严重后果。现有方法缺乏对这种问题的系统性检测。

Method: IAL-Scan首先将异构智能体代码抽象为框架无关的Agent IR，然后构建Agentic Loop Dependence Graph (ALDG)来恢复显式以及框架引入的反馈路径，最后检查这些路径是否能反复到达代价高昂或状态增长的操作且缺乏有效边界约束。

Result: 在6,549个LLM智能体仓库中，IAL-Scan报告了74个潜在发现，人工审查确认了分布在47个项目中的68个IAL故障，精确率达91.9%。

Conclusion: IAL-Scan能够有效检测LLM智能体项目中的无限循环故障，为保障智能体系统的可靠性和成本可控性提供了重要的静态分析手段。

Abstract: LLM agents increasingly rely on iterative execution to solve tasks through planning, tool use, state updates, and agent collaboration. While this design enables flexible automation, it also creates a new class of failures: an agent may repeatedly execute model calls, tools, workflow transitions, or agent handoffs when the feedback path is not effectively bounded. We call this problem Infinite Agentic Loops (IALs). IALs are not ordinary programming loops; they arise from the interaction between agent logic, framework semantics, runtime observations, and termination mechanisms. Such failures can amplify a single request into long running model and tool execution, causing cost exhaustion, model denial of service, context growth, and repeated external side effects. We propose IAL-Scan, a static analysis tool for detecting IAL failures in real-world LLM agent projects. IAL-Scan abstracts heterogeneous agent code into a framework independent Agent IR, builds an Agentic Loop Dependence Graph (ALDG) to recover explicit and framework induced feedback paths, and checks whether these paths can repeatedly reach costly or state growing operations without an effective bound. We evaluate IAL-Scan on 6,549 LLM agent repositories. It reports 74 potential findings, among which manual review confirms 68 IAL failures across 47 projects, achieving 91.9% precision.

</details>


### [208] [Refploit: Facilitating Exploit Construction via Code-Agent Trajectory Repair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01760)
*Zirui Chen,Zhipeng Xue,Jiayuan Zhou,Xing Hu,Xin Xia,Xiaohu Yang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Refploit提出了一种基于LLM的轨迹恢复框架，通过差分执行验证和约束引导的聚焦恢复，从公开的漏洞利用参考中自动复现可运行的漏洞利用代码，在143个Java漏洞的172个利用参考上实现了80.2%的复现率，相比初始轨迹相对提升64.3%，优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动从公开的漏洞利用参考中复现可运行的漏洞利用代码对于评估Java库漏洞的下游影响至关重要，但现有漏洞利用参考通常不完整、非结构化或仅描述部分步骤，而现有的代码智能体生成的漏洞利用常常表面上成功但实际上未触发真正的漏洞逻辑（如用自实现函数替换漏洞API）。

Method: Refploit首先通过差分执行验证智能体生成的漏洞利用是否有效；当无效时，分析复现进度，定位与复现进度相关的轨迹片段，并推导约束条件以引导聚焦恢复。核心思想是失败的智能体轨迹并非完全无用——它可能已完成部分子任务，同时揭示了应避免的误导性方向。

Result: 在三个开源Java漏洞数据集（143个漏洞的172个利用参考）上，基于DeepSeek-V4-Flash，Refploit成功复现138个漏洞利用，复现率80.2%；相比初始轨迹相对提升64.3%；优于SOTA方法PoCGen和高级代码智能体Codex with GPT-5.4。将其适配到另一个代码智能体上也观察到一致改进。

Conclusion: Refploit通过轨迹恢复策略有效提升了从公开参考中自动复现漏洞利用的成功率，且具有通用性，可适配不同的代码智能体。

Abstract: Vulnerability exploits play a crucial role in assessing the downstream impact of Java library vulnerabilities. While some vulnerabilities are accompanied by disclosed exploit references, automatically reproducing such references into runnable exploits remains challenging because they are often incomplete, unstructured, or only describe partial reproduction steps. Recent code agents provide a promising way to automate this process, but our study shows that their generated exploits often appear successful without triggering the actual vulnerable logic, such as replacing vulnerable APIs with self-implemented functions. To address this, we propose Refploit, an LLM-based trajectory recovery framework for facilitating vulnerability reproduction from public exploit references. The key insight is that a failed agent trajectory is not entirely useless. It may have already completed some reproduction subtasks while also revealing misleading directions that should be avoided. Refploit first validates an agent-generated exploit through differential execution. When the exploit is ineffective, Refploit analyzes its reproduction progress, locates the trajectory segments associated with the reproduction progress, and derives constraints to guide focused recovery. We evaluate Refploit on three open-source Java vulnerability datasets, covering 172 exploit references for 143 vulnerabilities. Under DeepSeek-V4-Flash, Refploit successfully reproduces 138 exploits, achieving a reproduction rate of 80.2%. It achieves a 64.3% relative improvement over the initially generated trajectories and outperforms both the SOTA exploit-generation method PoCGen and advanced code agents such as Codex with GPT-5.4.
